## Review findings — 2026-08-03 (M2 batch 2)

**Reviewer batch (adversarial review of PR #77 — L.B1.2 migration 066 draft core RPCs + pgTAP 020, `main...feat/M2-L.B1.2-draft-core`). VERDICT: FIX-THEN-MERGE — two should-fixes (each refuting a recorded claim via live probe), two nits; resolution on the SAME branch per house precedent (no new PR).**

### Should-fix

- **R122 · should-fix · 066:343-355 (× :645-653)** — `draft_start` racing an in-flight `draft_make_pick` deadlocks: the banner's "no cycle exists" claim (066:122-128, echoed in D105(5) and the PR body) misses the FK row lock. *Evidence:* live probe — session A took the drafts-row FOR UPDATE (the pick's step 1) then ran the pick's `draft_picks` INSERT; session B took the leagues-row FOR UPDATE then the drafts-row FOR UPDATE (draft_start's exact statements, which ran BEFORE the idempotent `started:false` return and before the scheduled gate). Result: `ERROR: deadlock detected … (40P01)`. The cycle: the pick's INSERT takes RI FOR KEY SHARE on the leagues row (`draft_picks.league_id` FK, 065:152), which conflicts with FOR UPDATE. Reachable by exactly the case the idempotent arm exists for — a double-clicked/retried Start while a pick is in flight; the victim can be the manager's pick, dying with a raw 40P01 instead of a friendly message. Self-healing, so not a blocker.
- **R123 · should-fix · 066:417-455** — `random` mode honors a stale SETTINGS-blob `draft_order`, so "random" silently isn't: the D101 "lobby showed it" rationale only covers the draft-row candidate, never the config fallback. *Evidence:* live probe — league with `draft_order_mode='random'`, no drafts row, and a leftover `settings.draft.draft_order` (as a prior manual/custom episode leaves; the Zod catalog accepts it and the settings PATCH stores it): draft_start → `started=true`, `drafts.draft_order` = the stale array VERBATIM, while the code's own md5 else-arm would have produced a different permutation. No lobby ever displayed a config-sourced order under random mode, so the recorded D105(2) justification is false for the fallback branch.

### Nit

### Should-fix

The clear-first exclusion (`.neq list_id`) only protects a byte-identical replay; this neighboring case runs `clearCurrentPrimary` before the natural-key 23505. Downstream: `is_primary_board` feeds L.B1.3's autopick — a silently lost primary degrades autopick to big-board/ADP.

## Review findings — 2026-08-04 (M2 batch 4)

**Reviewer batch (adversarial review of PR #79 — L.B1.3 migration 068 autopick + `draft_liveness`/`draft_touch` + `draft_tick` + pg_cron, `main...feat/M2-L.B1.3-autopick-tick`). VERDICT: FIX-THEN-MERGE — one should-fix (the D102 grace hold ended by the returning manager's own heartbeat, refuted via live probe), two nits (both taken); resolution on the SAME branch per house precedent (no new PR).**

### Should-fix

- **R132 · should-fix · supabase/migrations/068_draft_tick_autopick.sql:579-585** — a mid-hold reconnect ends the D102 grace hold instantly: the returning manager's own heartbeat flips them FRESH and the next tick autopicks them inside the grace window, instead of holding to deadline + grace with manual control restored. *Evidence:* live probe — stale on-clock seat, deadline 10s past, grace 30s → tick 1 `{held_for_grace: 1}`, 0 picks; then `draft_touch` as the returning manager; tick 2 → `{autopicked: 1}` with ~20s of hold remaining. The v2.8.13 §8.5.5 erratum this PR folded says the stale seat's pick "is held open until deadline + disconnect_grace_seconds — a returning manager may pick manually during the hold — then autopicks"; the code re-derived the branch each tick with freshness as of now(), so a post-deadline heartbeat retroactively granted the fresh-at-expiry branch. The manager got ≤5s — E3's "reconnect restores manual control" defeated in exactly the scenario the hold exists for. No 022 pin covered a post-deadline heartbeat (the 44s/46s pins both predate the deadline).

### Nit

- **R133 · nit · spec-redraft-leagues.md:1831 — TAKEN** — §22.3 still asserted "draft-tick keeps its immediate self-invocation after each user action" with no erratum marker; false post-D87. §8.2/§14 got the v2.8.13 marker; this restatement was missed.
- **R134 · nit · tasks-M2-snake-draft.md (§6 L.B7.1) — TAKEN** — F49's routing had no read-list line at the routed task (R125/R130 precedent): the fixture-instant sweep's landing site could start without finding the obligation.

### Resolution — 2026-08-04 (Builder, same branch `feat/M2-L.B1.3-autopick-tick`, PR #79)

| Finding | Resolved by |
|---|---|
| R132 (should-fix) | this commit — the fresh/stale branch is now decided from freshness **AS OF THE DEADLINE INSTANT**: a `draft_liveness` beat in `(deadline − draft_liveness_freshness(), deadline]`. The lower bound is STRICT so the pinned 45s constant keeps BOTH behavioral sides against the harness's 1s-past deadline (44s-old fresh / 46s-old stale — a `>=` bound would have flipped the 46s pin); the upper bound is what makes a mid-hold heartbeat unable to end the hold — it restores MANUAL control only (`draft_make_pick` never checks the deadline; the pick-3 manual-during-hold pin already covers that half). Pick-1's FRESH fixture pins its liveness instant PRE-deadline (`now() − 5s` — the txn-frozen now() had left the touch nominally post-deadline, which post-R132 correctly reads as a mid-hold beat; the scenario's intent is a heartbeat before expiry). **New reconnect-mid-hold pins (pgTAP 022 § pick 7, 67 → 72):** stale seat held at deadline+10s (baseline) → `draft_touch` as the returning manager (a post-deadline beat) → next tick STILL holds (0 picks + `held_for_grace ≥ 1`) → autopick lands only at deadline+grace+1s (`tk-rb06|autopick`). **Break probe shown:** branch reverted to the pre-fix now()-freshness form → fresh reset → EXACTLY tests 56–57 RED (56 `have: 1, want: 0` — the returning manager autopicked mid-hold) → reverted → 23 files/1312 green. RECORDED RESIDUAL (068 banner + D107 correction): the PK upsert keeps ONE row per (draft, user), so a beat landing between the deadline and the first tick OVERWRITES the pre-deadline evidence and that seat takes the hold — the error direction is the protective §8.5.5 hold, never an early autopick. |
| R133 (nit) | TAKEN — the §22.3 bullet gained the v2.8.13 erratum marker (same D87 vehicle erratum, missed site — no version bump), stating the in-database reality (no self-invocation; action RPCs set the next deadline synchronously, the ≤5s cron enforces expiry; pg_net = the escape hatch; the `idx_drafts_due` index stands). The v2.8.13 changelog entry's site list extended (§8.2/§14/**§22.3**) + a dated R133 note appended. PROGRESS header's spec pointer refreshed **v2.8.8 → v2.8.13** at the same touch (stale since v2.8.9 — bookkeeping, not a spec change). |
| R134 (nit) | TAKEN — tasks-M2 §6 **L.B7.1** gained the "Read ALSO PROGRESS §6 row F49" read-list line (the fixture-instant sweep + the four literal instants + "sweep to 2027+ BEFORE composing `test:gate:m1`, flip F49 in the same PR"); the F49 ledger row's discharge cell notes the routing (R51 findability, the R125/R130 precedent). |

**Recorded as an addendum to batch 5, not a new batch (re-review findings on the batch-5 remediation itself; resolution on the same branch/PR). Findings verbatim:**

Unreachable inside the ≤25-draft M2 gate, but the recorded rationale (068 banner, D108(9), PR body) is false in the adversarial case and neither the zombie class nor the >gate behavior is ledgered.
- **R142 · should-fix · tasks-M2-snake-draft.md (§6 L.B3.3 prompt)** — R137's "former member" render fallback is a hand-off named to L.B3.3 in D108(15) with no findability line (R51/R125/R130 precedent). ## Review findings — 2026-08-04 (M2 batch 6)

**Reviewer batch (adversarial review of PR #81 — L.B1.5 migration 070 Broadcast-from-DB + channel auth, `main...feat/M2-L.B1.5-realtime`). VERDICT: FIX-THEN-MERGE — one should-fix, three nits; resolution on the SAME branch per house precedent. Record kept deliberately minimal (the R144 lesson).**

- **R145 · should-fix · spec §12.14 (M4+ row)** — the rewritten trigger inventory omitted `team_week_results`, which §9's mechanism table and §9.1 both name as a trigger table; an M4 Builder using §12.14 as canonical would miss it. **Resolved:** the word added to the M4+ row — same v2.8.15 erratum completed, no version bump; dated note appended to the v2.8.15 changelog line.
- **R146 · nit · 068 ARM 3 — TAKEN** — the heartbeat's `'server_now', now()` was transaction_timestamp(), frozen at tick-txn start, so the drift-correction beat carried drift equal to the txn runtime. **Resolved:** `clock_timestamp()` (statement-time) + the reasoning comment at the send site. No pin asserted the old form (024 pins the payload KEYS + the deadline value; the wire test asserts type only) — none to update, 024 stays 63; the now-vs-clock delta itself stays deliberately unpinned (sub-ms in test txns — unpinnable without flake).
- **R147 · nit · `draft-realtime-db.test.ts` readiness gate — TAKEN** — the gate's worst-case budget (~224s = 8 × (10s join + 5 fires × 15 × 200ms polls + 3s backoff)) exceeded its own 180s beforeAll timeout, so terminal failure could surface as vitest's generic hook timeout instead of the gate's diagnostic error. **Resolved:** hook timeout raised to 300_000 (budget + setup slack; attempts/waits untouched — the gate's coverage is not trimmed); the arithmetic recorded in the comment.
- **R148 · nit — TAKEN** — the client-broadcast closure carried no forward pointer for M3. **Resolved:** one line appended to tasks-M2 §11's "M3 inherits" bullet: the presence-only `realtime.messages` INSERT posture (D109(5)/070 banner) is deliberate; M3's auction realtime decides whether to open Client Broadcast for bid-pulse/typing UX. No F-row per the reviewer's own ruling (R51 satisfied; the line is belt-and-suspenders).
